                          <button onClick={() => act(c, 'percent', Number(pct[c.id] ?? c.fidele_percent))} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-semibold">{t('Enregistrer le pourcentage')}</button>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex gap-4 text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mt-3 mb-2">
                    <span>{t('Visites :')} <b className="text-[#1a1a1a]">{c.visits_count}</b></span>
                    <span>{t('Total payé :')} <b className="text-[#1a1a1a]">{money(spent)}</b></span>
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#e4211b] mb-1.5">{t('Réparations précédentes')}</h4>
                  {h.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucun historique.')}</p>}
                  {h.map((o) => (
                    <div key={o.id} className="py-2 border-t border-[#f3f3ee] text-[13px]">
                      <div className="flex justify-between gap-2">
                        <b>{o.device_model}</b>
                        <span className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">{new Date(o.created_at).toLocaleDateString(getLocale())}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[#555]">{o.issue_description}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mt-0.5">
                        {o.order_number} · {STATUS[o.status] || o.status}
                        {o.final_price != null && ` · ${money(Number(o.final_price) - Number(o.discount || 0))}`}
                        {Number(o.discount_percent) > 0 && t('(remise {discount_percent} %)', { discount_percent: Number(o.discount_percent) })}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )
        })}
        {shown.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucun client.')}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/Debts.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

const KINDS = {
  client_unpaid: { label: t('Dettes des clients (impayées)'), party: t('Nom du client'), dir: 'receivable' },
  supplier_unpaid: { label: t('Je dois aux fournisseurs (achats non payés)'), party: t('Nom du fournisseur / vendeur'), dir: 'payable' },
  money_lent: { label: t('Argent que j\'ai avancé (non remboursé)'), party: t('Nom de la personne'), dir: 'receivable' },
}

// One page for comptoir (client debts only) and admin (all 3 kinds).
// RLS already hides the other kinds from comptoir; we also hide the tabs.
export default function Debts() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const isAdmin = profile?.role === 'admin' || profile?.role === 'super_admin'
  const kinds = isAdmin ? Object.keys(KINDS) : ['client_unpaid']
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('client_unpaid')
  const [debts, setDebts] = useState([])
  const [showSettled, setShowSettled] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ party_name: '', description: '', amount: '', due_date: '', from_register: false })
  const [payingId, setPayingId] = useState(null)
  const [pay, setPay] = useState({ amount: '', fromRegister: false })

  useEffect(() => { if (user) load() }, [user, tab, showSettled])

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)
    let q = supabase.from('debts')
      .select('id, party_name, description, amount, paid_amount, status, due_date, from_register, created_at')
      .eq('kind', tab).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    if (!showSettled) q = q.eq('status', 'open')
    const { data, error: e } = await q
    if (e) setError(e.message)
    setDebts(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  async function addDebt(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(null)
    if (!form.party_name.trim() || !form.description.trim() || Number(form.amount) <= 0) {
      setError(t('Remplissez correctement le nom, la description et le montant'))
      return
    }
    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('debts').insert({
      kind: tab, party_name: form.party_name.trim(), description: form.description.trim(),
      amount: Number(form.amount), due_date: form.due_date || null, created_by: user.id,
      from_register: tab === 'money_lent' && form.from_register,
    })
    if (err) { setError(t('Erreur :') + ' ' + err.message); return }
    setForm({ party_name: '', description: '', amount: '', due_date: '', from_register: false })
    setShowForm(false)
    load()
  }

  async function submitPayment(d) {
    const { error: err } = await supabase.rpc('add_debt_payment', {
      p_debt: d.id, p_amount: Number(pay.amount), p_from_register: pay.fromRegister, p_note: null,
    })
    if (err) { alert(t('Échec :') + ' ' + err.message); return }
    setPayingId(null)
    setPay({ amount: '', fromRegister: false })
    load()
  }

  const remaining = (d) => Number(d.amount) - Number(d.paid_amount)
  const total = debts.reduce((s, d) => s + remaining(d), 0)
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
  const inp = 'w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]'

  // Shared "pay / collect" control — same markup used inside the mobile
  // card and the desktop table cell, so the two never drift apart.
  function PaymentControl({ d }) {
    if (d.status !== 'open') return null
    if (payingId !== d.id) {
      return (
        <button
          onClick={() => { setPayingId(d.id); setPay({ amount: remaining(d).toFixed(3), fromRegister: false }) }}
          className="bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg"
        >
          {KINDS[tab].dir === 'payable' ? t('Régler') : t('Recevoir un paiement')}
        </button>
      )
    }
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:w-40">
        <input
          type="number" step="0.001" placeholder={t('Montant')} value={pay.amount}
          onChange={(e) => setPay({ ...pay, amount: e.target.value })}
          className="px-2.5 py-1.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-xs"
        />
        {tab === 'supplier_unpaid' && (
          <label className="text-xs flex gap-1.5 items-center">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={pay.fromRegister}
              onChange={(e) => setPay({ ...pay, fromRegister: e.target.checked })} />  {t('De la caisse')}
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => submitPayment(d)} className="bg-[#1f8a4c] text-white text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg">{t('Enregistrer')}</button>
          <button onClick={() => setPayingId(null)} className="text-xs px-2">×</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  function StatusPill({ d }) {
    const late = d.status === 'open' && d.due_date && d.due_date < today
    if (d.status === 'settled') return <span className="text-[#1f8a4c] font-semibold text-xs">{t('✔ Soldée')}</span>
    return (
      <span className={`text-xs font-semibold ${late ? 'text-[#b3170f]' : 'text-[#c9750a]'}`}>
        {late ? t('En retard') + ' ' : t('Ouvert') + ' '}{d.due_date || ''}
      </span>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-end mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Dettes')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">
            {KINDS[tab].dir === 'payable' ? t('Reste à payer :') + ' ' : t('Reste à recevoir :') + ' '}
            <b>{total.toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}</b>
          </p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}
          className="bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg self-start sm:self-auto">
          {showForm ? t('Annuler') : t('+ Enregistrer une dette')}
        </button>
      </div>

      {kinds.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-5 overflow-x-auto -mx-1 px-1 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0 sm:flex-wrap">
          {kinds.map((k) => (
            <button key={k} onClick={() => { setTab(k); setShowForm(false) }}
              className={`shrink-0 text-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border ${tab === k ? 'bg-[#e4211b] text-white border-[#e4211b]' : 'border-[#e5e5e5]'}`}>
              {KINDS[k].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="bg-[#fdeaea] text-[#b3170f] text-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-4">{error}</p>}

      {showForm && (
        <form onSubmit={addDebt} className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-6 mb-6 max-w-lg">
          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{KINDS[tab].party}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-4'} value={form.party_name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, party_name: e.target.value })} />
          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Pour quoi ? (réparation / accessoire / marchandise / avance...)')}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-4'} value={form.description} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Montant')}</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.001" className={inp} value={form.amount} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, amount: e.target.value })} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Date d\'échéance (facultatif)')}</label>
              <input type="date" className={inp} value={form.due_date} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, due_date: e.target.value })} />
            </div>
          </div>
          {tab === 'money_lent' && (
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm mb-3 bg-[#fff8f0] border border-[#f0d9b8] rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={form.from_register}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, from_register: e.target.checked })} />
              
              {t('L\'argent est sorti de la caisse (il est déduit de la caisse tout de suite, et revient quand la personne le rembourse)')}
            </label>
          )}
          {tab === 'supplier_unpaid' && (
            <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-3">
              
              {t('Attention : n\'enregistrez pas le même achat dans « Dépenses », sinon il sera compté deux fois. La dette est ici ; quand vous la payez, une sortie de caisse est enregistrée (si vous le choisissez).')}
            </p>
          )}
          <button className="w-full sm:w-auto bg-[#e4211b] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg">{t('Enregistrer')}</button>
        </form>
      )}

      <label className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={showSettled} onChange={(e) => setShowSettled(e.target.checked)} />  {t('Afficher les dettes soldées')}
      </label>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl">
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p>
        </div>
      ) : debts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl">
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucune dette ouverte')}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Phone / tablet: one card per debt */}
          <div className="lg:hidden flex flex-col gap-3">
            {debts.map((d) => (
              <div key={d.id} className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-3 mb-2">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-[#1a1a1a] text-sm">{d.party_name}</p>
                    {tab === 'money_lent' && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-[#6b6b6b]">{d.from_register ? t('De la caisse') : t('De ma poche')}</p>
                    )}
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mt-0.5 break-words">{d.description}</p>
                  </div>
                  <StatusPill d={d} />
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs bg-[#faf9f5] rounded-lg p-2.5 mb-3">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Montant')}</p>
                    <p className="font-semibold">{Number(d.amount).toFixed(2)}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Payé')}</p>
                    <p className="font-semibold">{Number(d.paid_amount).toFixed(2)}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Reste')}</p>
                    <p className="font-bold">{remaining(d).toFixed(2)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <PaymentControl d={d} />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Desktop: the original table */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-[#faf9f5] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] font-semibold">
                  {[t('Nom'), t('Pour quoi'), t('Montant'), t('Payé'), t('Reste'), t('Statut / échéance'), ''].map((t) => (
                    <th key={t} className="text-start px-4 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {debts.map((d) => (
                  <tr key={d.id} className="hover:bg-[#fbfaf6] align-top">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] font-semibold">
                      {d.party_name}
                      {tab === 'money_lent' && (
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-[#6b6b6b] font-normal">{d.from_register ? t('De la caisse') : t('De ma poche')}</div>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] text-xs">{d.description}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">{Number(d.amount).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">{Number(d.paid_amount).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] font-bold">{remaining(d).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]"><StatusPill d={d} /></td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]"><PaymentControl d={d} /></td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/GeneralExpenses.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Shop-wide spending NOT tied to a repair (water, paper, taxi...).
// Available to admin, comptoir and technicien. Needs admin approval.
export default function GeneralExpenses() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)

  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) loadExpenses()
  }, [user])

  async function loadExpenses() {
    setLoading(true)
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('general_expenses')
      .select('id, description, amount, is_approved, created_at')
      .eq('spent_by', user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setExpenses(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(null)

    if (!description.trim() || !amount || Number(amount) <= 0) {
      setError(t('Remplissez correctement la description et le montant'))
      return
    }

    setSaving(true)
    const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('general_expenses').insert({
      spent_by: user.id,
      description: description.trim(),
      amount: Number(amount),
    })
    setSaving(false)

    if (insertError) {
      setError(t('Erreur :') + ' ' + insertError.message)
      return
    }

    setDescription('')
    setAmount('')
    setShowForm(false)
    loadExpenses()
  }

  const totalPending = expenses
    .filter((e) => !e.is_approved)
    .reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount), 0)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Dépenses générales')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">
            {totalPending > 0
              ? t('{totalPending} DT en attente de validation par le gérant', { totalPending: totalPending.toFixed(2) })
              : t('Toutes vos dépenses sont validées')}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}
          className="bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90"
        >
          {showForm ? t('Annuler') : t('+ Enregistrer une dépense')}
        </button>
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <form
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
          className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-6 mb-6 max-w-lg"
        >
          <div className="mb-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Description de la dépense (pour quoi avez-vous dépensé ?)')}</label>
            <input
              value={description}
              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t('Exemple : bouteille d\'eau, papier d\'impression, taxi...')}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Montant')}</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="0.001"
              value={amount}
              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t('En dinars')}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {error && <p className="text-[#b3170f] text-sm mb-3">{error}</p>}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={saving}
            className="bg-[#e4211b] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {saving ? t('Enregistrement...') : t('Ajouter')}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p>
        ) : expenses.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucune dépense enregistrée pour le moment')}</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-[#faf9f5] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] font-semibold">
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Description')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Montant')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Statut')}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {expenses.map((e) => (
                <tr key={e.id} className="hover:bg-[#fbfaf6]">
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] font-semibold">{e.description}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">{Number(e.amount).toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">
                    {e.is_approved ? (
                      <span className="text-[#1f8a4c] text-xs font-semibold">{t('✔ Validée')}</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-[#c9750a] text-xs font-semibold">{t('En attente de validation')}</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/LogRepairExpense.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// One page for comptoir, technicien AND admin.
// Comptoir/admin can attach a part purchase to any open order;
// a technician only sees the orders assigned to them.
export default function LogRepairExpense() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState([])
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)

  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState('')
  const [itemName, setItemName] = useState('')
  const [supplier, setSupplier] = useState('')
  const [price, setPrice] = useState('')
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState('1')
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && profile) {
      loadExpenses()
      loadOpenOrders()
    }
  }, [user, profile])

  async function loadExpenses() {
    setLoading(true)
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('repair_expenses')
      .select(
        'id, item_name, supplier, purchase_price, quantity, is_approved, created_at, repair_orders(order_number, device_model)'
      )
      .eq('spent_by', user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setExpenses(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  async function loadOpenOrders() {
    let query = supabase
      .from('repair_orders')
      .select('id, order_number, device_model')
      .not('status', 'in', '(delivered,cancelled)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })

    if (profile?.role === 'technicien') {
      query = query.eq('technician_id', user.id)
    }

    const { data } = await query
    setOrders(data || [])
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(null)

    if (!orderId || !itemName.trim() || !price || Number(price) <= 0) {
      setError(t('Choisissez la commande et remplissez correctement le nom de la pièce et le prix'))
      return
    }

    setSaving(true)
    const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('repair_expenses').insert({
      repair_order_id: orderId,
      spent_by: user.id,
      technician_id: profile?.role === 'technicien' ? user.id : null,
      item_name: itemName.trim(),
      supplier: supplier.trim() || null,
      purchase_price: Number(price),
      quantity: Number(quantity) || 1,
    })
    setSaving(false)

    if (insertError) {
      setError(t('Erreur :') + ' ' + insertError.message)
      return
    }

    setOrderId('')
    setItemName('')
    setSupplier('')
    setPrice('')
    setQuantity('1')
    setShowForm(false)
    loadExpenses()
  }

  const totalPending = expenses
    .filter((e) => !e.is_approved)
    .reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.purchase_price * e.quantity, 0)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Frais de pièces détachées')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">
            {totalPending > 0
              ? t('{totalPending} DT en attente de validation par le gérant', { totalPending: totalPending.toFixed(2) })
              : t('Toutes vos dépenses sont validées')}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}
          className="bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90"
        >
          {showForm ? t('Annuler') : t('+ Enregistrer une dépense')}
        </button>
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <form
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
          className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-6 mb-6 max-w-xl"
        >
          <div className="mb-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Liée à une commande de réparation')}</label>
            <select
              value={orderId}
              onChange={(e) => setOrderId(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm"
              required
            >
              <option value="">{t('Choisir la commande...')}</option>
              {orders.map((o) => (
                <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                  {o.order_number} — {o.device_model}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Nom de la pièce')}</label>
            <input
              value={itemName}
              onChange={(e) => setItemName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t('Exemple : écran iPhone 12, batterie...')}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              required
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Fournisseur (facultatif)')}</label>
              <input
                value={supplier}
                onChange={(e) => setSupplier(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Quantité')}</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={quantity}
                onChange={(e) => setQuantity(e.target.value)}
                min="1"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Prix d\'achat (à l\'unité)')}</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="0.001"
              value={price}
              onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t('En dinars')}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {error && <p className="text-[#b3170f] text-sm mb-3">{error}</p>}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={saving}
            className="bg-[#e4211b] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {saving ? t('Enregistrement...') : t('Ajouter')}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p>
        ) : expenses.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucune dépense enregistrée pour le moment')}</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-[#faf9f5] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] font-semibold">
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Commande')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Pièce')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Quantité')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Prix')}</th>
                <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Statut')}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {expenses.map((e) => (
                <tr key={e.id} className="hover:bg-[#fbfaf6]">
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">
                    {e.repair_orders?.order_number} — {e.repair_orders?.device_model}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] font-semibold">{e.item_name}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">{e.quantity}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">
                    {(e.purchase_price * e.quantity).toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">
                    {e.is_approved ? (
                      <span className="text-[#1f8a4c] text-xs font-semibold">{t('✔ Validée')}</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-[#c9750a] text-xs font-semibold">{t('En attente de validation')}</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/LowStockAlert.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Shows a small warning banner for accessories running low on stock.
// Phones are excluded on purpose: each phone is a single unique unit (IMEI),
// so "low stock" has no meaning for them — they're either in stock (1) or sold (0).
export default function LowStockAlert() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  async function load() {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('accessories')
      .select('id, name, stock_quantity, low_stock_threshold')
      .eq('category', 'accessory')
      .eq('is_deleted', false)
      .order('stock_quantity', { ascending: true })
    setItems((data || []).filter((a) => a.stock_quantity <= (a.low_stock_threshold ?? 3)))
    setLoading(false)
  }

  if (loading || items.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#fff8f0] border border-[#f0d9b8] rounded-xl p-4 mb-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#8a4f06]">{t('⚠ Stock faible (')}{items.length})</p>
          <ul className="text-xs text-[#8a4f06] mt-1.5 space-y-0.5">
            {items.slice(0, 5).map((a) => (
              <li key={a.id}>
                {a.name} — {t('Reste')} {a.stock_quantity} {a.stock_quantity === 0 && t('(épuisé)')}
              </li>
            ))}
            {items.length > 5 && <li>{t('et {n} autres produits...', { n: items.length - 5 })}</li>}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <Link to="/admin/accessories" className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold text-[#8a4f06] underline whitespace-nowrap">
          
          {t('Gestion du stock')}
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/MyAccount.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import PasswordField from '../../components/auth/PasswordField'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

const ROLE_LABEL = { admin: 'Admin', super_admin: 'Super Admin', comptoir: 'Comptoir', technicien: 'Technicien' }
const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/

function Card({ icon, title, desc, children }) {
  return (
    <section className="bg-white border border-[#e8e6df] rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 mb-5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
        <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#f4f2ec] flex items-center justify-center text-lg shrink-0">{icon}</span>
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a]">{title}</h2>
          {desc && <p className="text-[13px] text-[#6b6b6b] mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">{desc}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  )
}

function Msg({ m }) {
  if (!m) return null
  const ok = m.type === 'ok'
  return (
    <p className={`text-sm rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 mb-4 leading-relaxed ${ok ? 'bg-[#eaf7ee] text-[#17692f]' : 'bg-[#fdecea] text-[#b3170f]'}`}>{m.text}</p>
  )
}

// Verifies the current password without touching the open session's result for the UI.
async function checkPassword(email, password) {
  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password })
  return !error
}

export default function MyAccount() {
  const { user, profile, logout } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(user?.email || '')
  const [pendingEmail, setPendingEmail] = useState(user?.new_email || null)

  // password form
  const [curPw, setCurPw] = useState('')
  const [newPw, setNewPw] = useState('')
  const [newPw2, setNewPw2] = useState('')
  const [pwMsg, setPwMsg] = useState(null)
  const [pwBusy, setPwBusy] = useState(false)

  // email form
  const [newEmail, setNewEmail] = useState('')
  const [emPw, setEmPw] = useState('')
  const [emMsg, setEmMsg] = useState(null)
  const [emBusy, setEmBusy] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {
      if (data?.user) { setEmail(data.user.email || ''); setPendingEmail(data.user.new_email || null) }
    })
  }, [])

  async function changePassword(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setPwMsg(null)
    if (newPw.length < 6) return setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Le nouveau mot de passe doit contenir au moins 6 caractères') })
    if (newPw !== newPw2) return setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Les deux nouveaux mots de passe ne correspondent pas') })
    if (newPw === curPw) return setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Choisissez un mot de passe différent de l\'actuel') })
    setPwBusy(true)
    if (!(await checkPassword(email, curPw))) {
      setPwBusy(false)
      return setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Le mot de passe actuel est incorrect') })
    }
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: newPw })
    setPwBusy(false)
    if (error) return setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Une erreur est survenue :') + ' ' + error.message })
    setCurPw(''); setNewPw(''); setNewPw2('')
    setPwMsg({ type: 'ok', text: t('Mot de passe modifié ✅') })
  }

  async function changeEmail(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setEmMsg(null)
    const clean = newEmail.trim().toLowerCase()
    if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(clean)) return setEmMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Format d\'e-mail incorrect') })
    if (clean === email.toLowerCase()) return setEmMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('C\'est déjà votre e-mail actuel') })
    setEmBusy(true)
    if (!(await checkPassword(email, emPw))) {
      setEmBusy(false)
      return setEmMsg({ type: 'err', text: t('Mot de passe incorrect') })
    }
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ email: clean }, { emailRedirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/account` })
    setEmBusy(false)
    if (error) {
      const m = error.message || ''
      return setEmMsg({ type: 'err', text: m.toLowerCase().includes('already') ? t('Cet e-mail est déjà utilisé par un autre compte') : t('Une erreur est survenue :') + ' ' + m })
    }
    setPendingEmail(clean)
    setNewEmail(''); setEmPw('')
    setEmMsg({ type: 'ok', text: t('Nous avons envoyé un lien de confirmation. Ouvrez-le (et si demandé, ouvrez aussi le lien reçu sur l\'ancien e-mail). L\'e-mail ne change qu\'après confirmation, et vous continuez à vous connecter avec l\'ancien d\'ici là.') })
  }

  async function handleLogout() {
    await logout()
    navigate('/login')
  }

  const initial = (profile?.full_name || '?').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase()
  const inputCls = 'w-full h-12 px-4 border border-[#e2e0d8] rounded-xl text-base bg-[#fdfdfb] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#e4211b]/10 transition'
  const btnCls = 'w-full sm:w-auto h-12 px-7 bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-[15px] hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-60 active:scale-[0.99] transition'

  return (
    <div className="max-w-xl">
      {/* identity card */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-[#1f1f1f] to-[#2b2b2b] text-white rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 mb-5 flex items-center gap-4">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-[#e4211b] text-2xl font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">{initial}</div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-lg font-extrabold truncate">{profile?.full_name || '—'}</p>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[#c9c9c9] truncate" dir="ltr">{email}</p>
          <span className="inline-block mt-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold bg-white/10 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5">{ROLE_LABEL[profile?.role] || profile?.role}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Card icon="🔑" title={t('Changer le mot de passe')} desc={t('Vous devez saisir votre mot de passe actuel pour confirmer.')}>
        <form onSubmit={changePassword}>
          <PasswordField label={t('Mot de passe actuel')} value={curPw} onChange={setCurPw} autoComplete="current-password" />
          <PasswordField label={t('Nouveau mot de passe')} value={newPw} onChange={setNewPw} autoComplete="new-password" hint={t('6 caractères minimum')} />
          <PasswordField label={t('Saisissez le nouveau à nouveau')} value={newPw2} onChange={setNewPw2} autoComplete="new-password" />
          <Msg m={pwMsg} />
          <button type="submit" disabled={pwBusy} className={btnCls}>{pwBusy ? t('Enregistrement...') : t('Changer le mot de passe')}</button>
        </form>
        <p className="text-xs text-[#8a8a8a] mt-4 leading-relaxed">{t('Mot de passe actuel oublié ? Appuyez sur « Se déconnecter » ci-dessous, puis sur la page de connexion choisissez « Mot de passe oublié ? ». Ou demandez à l\'administrateur de le changer pour vous.')}</p>
      </Card>

      <Card icon="✉️" title={t('Changer l\'e-mail')} desc={t('L\'e-mail est ce avec quoi vous vous connectez. Nous enverrons un lien de confirmation.')}>
        {pendingEmail && (
          <p className="text-[13px] bg-[#fff8e6] border border-[#f0d58a] text-[#8a6d1d] rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 mb-4" dir="ltr">
            Waiting for confirmation: <b>{pendingEmail}</b>
          </p>
        )}
        <form onSubmit={changeEmail}>
          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1.5">{t('Nouvel e-mail')}</label>
          <input type="email" dir="ltr" value={newEmail} onChange={(e) => setNewEmail(e.target.value)} autoComplete="email" required className={`${inputCls} mb-4`} />
          <PasswordField label={t('Mot de passe (pour confirmer)')} value={emPw} onChange={setEmPw} autoComplete="current-password" />
          <Msg m={emMsg} />
          <button type="submit" disabled={emBusy} className={btnCls}>{emBusy ? t('Envoi en cours...') : t('Changer l\'e-mail')}</button>
        </form>
      </Card>

      <button onClick={handleLogout} className="w-full h-12 border border-[#e2e0d8] bg-white text-[#b3170f] font-bold rounded-xl text-[15px] hover:bg-[#fdecea] transition mb-6">
        
        {t('Se déconnecter')}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/PriceGuide.jsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t, getLocale } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Common part types shown as quick filter chips + suggestions in the form.
// Free text is still allowed (e.g. "بورت شحن") — this list is just a shortcut.
const COMMON_PARTS = [t('Écran'), t('Batterie'), t('Écouteur'), t('Caméra'), t('Port de charge'), t('Vitre arrière')]
// part names are typed by the team, so the icon is found for the Arabic (old data), French and English words
const PART_ICONS = {
  'شاشة': '🖥️', 'بطارية': '🔋', 'سماعة': '🔊', 'كاميرا': '📷', 'بورت شحن': '🔌', 'زجاج خلفي': '🪟',
  'Écran': '🖥️', 'Batterie': '🔋', 'Écouteur': '🔊', 'Caméra': '📷', 'Port de charge': '🔌', 'Vitre arrière': '🪟',
  'Screen': '🖥️', 'Battery': '🔋', 'Earpiece': '🔊', 'Camera': '📷', 'Charging port': '🔌', 'Back glass': '🪟',
}
const partIcon = (type) => PART_ICONS[type] || '🔧'

function priceLabel(r) {
  const hasMax = r.price_max != null && Number(r.price_max) !== Number(r.price_min)
  return hasMax
    ? t('{price_min} - {price_max} DT', { price_min: Number(r.price_min).toFixed(2), price_max: Number(r.price_max).toFixed(2) })
    : t('{price_min} DT', { price_min: Number(r.price_min).toFixed(2) })
}

export default function PriceGuide() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  // Admin AND technicien can add / edit / delete. Comptoir can only view and search.
  const canEdit = ['admin', 'super_admin', 'technicien'].includes(profile?.role)

  const [rows, setRows] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [partFilter, setPartFilter] = useState('')

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm())
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState(null)

  function emptyForm() {
    return { device_model: '', part_type: '', quality_label: '', price_min: '', price_max: '', notes: '' }
  }

  useEffect(() => { load() }, [])

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)
    // two foreign keys to profiles (created_by / updated_by) -> name the one we want
    const { data, error: e } = await supabase
      .from('part_price_guide')
      .select(
        'id, device_model, part_type, quality_label, price_min, price_max, notes, updated_at, ' +
        'profiles!part_price_guide_updated_by_fkey(full_name)'
      )
      .order('device_model', { ascending: true })
      .order('part_type', { ascending: true })
      .order('price_min', { ascending: true })
    if (e) setError(e.message)
    setRows(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  // Search box filters by device model OR part type OR quality label — all client-side,
  // since the whole guide is small enough to load once and filter instantly as you type.
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase()
    return rows.filter((r) => {
      const matchesText =
        !q ||
        r.device_model.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        r.part_type.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        r.quality_label.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      const matchesPart = !partFilter || r.part_type === partFilter
      return matchesText && matchesPart
    })
  }, [rows, search, partFilter])

  // group by device model so all rows for "iPhone 13" (screen x3, battery...) sit together
  const grouped = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map()
    for (const r of filtered) {
      if (!map.has(r.device_model)) map.set(r.device_model, [])
      map.get(r.device_model).push(r)
    }
    return [...map.entries()]
  }, [filtered])

  const partTypesInUse = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(rows.map((r) => r.part_type))].sort(),
    [rows]
  )

  function openAdd() {
    setEditingId(null)
    setForm(emptyForm())
    setFormError(null)
    setShowForm(true)
  }

  function openEdit(r) {
    setEditingId(r.id)
    setForm({
      device_model: r.device_model, part_type: r.part_type, quality_label: r.quality_label,
      price_min: r.price_min, price_max: r.price_max ?? '', notes: r.notes || '',
    })
    setFormError(null)
    setShowForm(true)
  }

  async function submitForm(e) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setFormError(null)
    const min = Number(form.price_min)
    const max = form.price_max === '' ? null : Number(form.price_max)
    if (!form.device_model.trim() || !form.part_type.trim() || !form.quality_label.trim() || min <= 0) {
      setFormError(t('Remplissez le modèle, le type de pièce, la qualité et au moins le prix « de »'))
      return
    }
    if (max !== null && max < min) {
      setFormError(t('« À » doit être supérieur ou égal à « De »'))
      return
    }
    setSaving(true)
    const payload = {
      device_model: form.device_model.trim(),
      part_type: form.part_type.trim(),
      quality_label: form.quality_label.trim(),
      price_min: min,
      price_max: max,
      notes: form.notes.trim() || null,
    }
    const { error: err } = editingId
      ? await supabase.from('part_price_guide').update(payload).eq('id', editingId)
      : await supabase.from('part_price_guide').insert({ ...payload, created_by: user.id })
    setSaving(false)
    if (err) { setFormError(t('Erreur :') + ' ' + err.message); return }
    setShowForm(false)
    load()
  }

  async function remove(id) {
    if (!window.confirm(t('Supprimer définitivement ce prix ?'))) return
    const { error: err } = await supabase.from('part_price_guide').delete().eq('id', id)
    if (err) { alert(t('Échec :') + ' ' + err.message); return }
    load()
  }

  const inp = 'w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]'

  function EditedBy({ r }) {
    if (!r.updated_at) return null
    return (
      <p className="text-[10px] text-[#9a9a9a] mt-1">
        {t('Dernière modification')} : {r.profiles?.full_name || '—'} · {new Date(r.updated_at).toLocaleDateString(getLocale())}
      </p>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-end mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Guide des prix')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Prix estimatifs (de - à) selon le modèle du téléphone et le type de pièce')}</p>
        </div>
        {canEdit && (
          <button onClick={showForm ? () => setShowForm(false) : openAdd}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg">
            {showForm ? t('Annuler') : t('+ Ajouter un prix')}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* search + part-type chips */}
      <div className="mb-5">
        <input
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={t('Rechercher par modèle de téléphone ou type de pièce (ex. : iPhone 13 ou écran)')}
          className={inp + ' mb-3'}
        />
        {partTypesInUse.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto -mx-1 px-1">
            <button onClick={() => setPartFilter('')}
              className={`shrink-0 text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border ${!partFilter ? 'bg-[#e4211b] text-white border-[#e4211b]' : 'border-[#e5e5e5] text-[#6b6b6b]'}`}>
              
              {t('Tout')}
            </button>
            {partTypesInUse.map((p) => (
              <button key={p} onClick={() => setPartFilter(p === partFilter ? '' : p)}
                className={`shrink-0 text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border flex items-center gap-1.5 ${partFilter === p ? 'bg-[#e4211b] text-white border-[#e4211b]' : 'border-[#e5e5e5] text-[#6b6b6b]'}`}>
                <span>{partIcon(p)}</span>{p}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {error && <p className="bg-[#fdeaea] text-[#b3170f] text-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-4">{error}</p>}

      {canEdit && showForm && (
        <form onSubmit={submitForm} className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-6 mb-6 max-w-lg">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm mb-4">{editingId ? t('Modifier le prix') : t('Nouveau prix')}</h3>
          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Modèle du téléphone')}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-4'} placeholder={t('Ex. : iPhone 13 Pro')}
            value={form.device_model} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, device_model: e.target.value })} />

          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Type de pièce')}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-2'} placeholder={t('Ex. : écran')}
            value={form.part_type} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, part_type: e.target.value })} list="part-type-options" />
          <datalist id="part-type-options">
            {COMMON_PARTS.map((p) => <option key={p} value={p} />)}
          </datalist>
          <div className="flex gap-1.5 flex-wrap mb-4">
            {COMMON_PARTS.map((p) => (
              <button type="button" key={p} onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, part_type: p })}
                className="text-[11px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border border-[#e5e5e5] text-[#6b6b6b] hover:border-[#e4211b] hover:text-[#e4211b]">
                {p}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Qualité / type (original, copie 1er choix...)')}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-4'} placeholder={t('Ex. : original')}
            value={form.quality_label} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, quality_label: e.target.value })} />

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('De (DT)')}</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.001" className={inp}
                value={form.price_min} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, price_min: e.target.value })} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('À (DT) — facultatif')}</label>
              <input type="number" step="0.001" className={inp} placeholder={t('Laissez vide si le prix est fixe')}
                value={form.price_max} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, price_max: e.target.value })} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Remarques (facultatif)')}</label>
          <input className={inp + ' mb-4'}
            value={form.notes} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })} />

          {formError && <p className="text-[#b3170f] text-sm mb-3">{formError}</p>}
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button disabled={saving} className="bg-[#e4211b] text-white font-semibold text-sm px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
              {saving ? '...' : t('Enregistrer')}
            </button>
            {editingId && (
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { remove(editingId); setShowForm(false) }}
                className="text-[#b3170f] text-sm px-4 py-2.5">
                
                {t('Supprimer')}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl"><p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p></div>
      ) : grouped.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl">
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">
            {rows.length === 0 ? t('Aucun prix enregistré pour le moment.') : t('Aucun résultat pour cette recherche.')}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {grouped.map(([model, items]) => (
            <div key={model} className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-5 py-3 bg-[#faf9f5] border-b border-[#e5e5e5] border-e-[3px] border-e-[#e4211b]">
                <span className="font-bold text-[#1a1a1a] text-sm">{model}</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-[#9a9a9a]">{t('{n} prix', { n: items.length })}</span>
              </div>

              {/* phone: stacked rows */}
              <div className="lg:hidden divide-y divide-[#f0f0ea]">
                {items.map((r) => (
                  <div key={r.id} className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex gap-2.5">
                      <span className="text-base shrink-0">{partIcon(r.part_type)}</span>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#1a1a1a]">{r.part_type} — {r.quality_label}</p>
                        {r.notes && <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mt-0.5">{r.notes}</p>}
                        <EditedBy r={r} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-start shrink-0 flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5">
                      <span className="bg-[#eefaf1] text-[#1f8a4c] font-bold text-xs whitespace-nowrap px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                        {priceLabel(r)}
                      </span>
                      {canEdit && (
                        <button onClick={() => openEdit(r)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-[#6b6b6b] border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg px-2 py-1 hover:border-[#e4211b] hover:text-[#e4211b] transition-colors">
                          
                          {t('✏️ Modifier')}
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* desktop: table */}
              <table className="w-full text-sm hidden lg:table">
                <tbody>
                  {items.map((r) => (
                    <tr key={r.id} className="hover:bg-[#fbfaf6] border-b border-[#f0f0ea] last:border-b-0">
                      <td className="px-5 py-3 w-48 font-semibold">
                        <span className="ms-1.5">{partIcon(r.part_type)}</span>{r.part_type}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3 text-[#6b6b6b]">{r.quality_label}</td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3 text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">
                        {r.notes}
                        <EditedBy r={r} />
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3">
                        <span className="bg-[#eefaf1] text-[#1f8a4c] font-bold text-xs whitespace-nowrap px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full">
                          {priceLabel(r)}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      {canEdit && (
                        <td className="px-5 py-3 text-start">
                          <button onClick={() => openEdit(r)}
                            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 hover:border-[#e4211b] hover:text-[#e4211b] transition-colors">
                            
                            {t('✏️ Modifier')}
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      )}
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/ProductScanRedirect.jsx

import { Navigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Opened by scanning a stock label: /p/<product id>
// comptoir -> sale dialog for that product; admin -> its edit sheet; technician -> a short message.
export default function ProductScanRedirect() {
  const { productId } = useParams()
  const { profile } = useAuth()
  if (!profile) return <p style={{ padding: 40 }}>{t('Ouverture...')}</p>
  if (profile.role === 'comptoir') return <Navigate to={`/comptoir/sell?item=${productId}`} replace />
  if (profile.role === 'admin' || profile.role === 'super_admin') return <Navigate to={`/admin/accessories?item=${productId}`} replace />
  return <p style={{ padding: 40 }}>{t('Ceci est une étiquette de produit en vente — elle s\'ouvre à la caisse ou chez l\'administrateur.')}</p>
}

src/pages/shared/ScanRedirect.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Navigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Opened by scanning the sticker on a device: /o/ORD12345678
// technicien / admin -> the repair details page; comptoir -> the orders list pre-filtered.
export default function ScanRedirect() {
  const { orderNumber } = useParams()
  const { profile } = useAuth()
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(null)
  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!profile) return
    if (profile.role === 'comptoir') {
      setTarget(`/comptoir?q=${encodeURIComponent(orderNumber)}`)
      return
    }
    supabase.from('repair_orders').select('id').eq('order_number', orderNumber).maybeSingle()
      .then(({ data }) => (data ? setTarget(`/technicien/order/${data.id}`) : setNotFound(true)))
  }, [profile, orderNumber])

  if (target) return <Navigate to={target} replace />
  if (notFound) return <p style={{ padding: 40 }}>{t('Commande')} {orderNumber}  {t('introuvable ou non assigné à vous')}</p>
  return <p style={{ padding: 40 }}>{t('Ouverture...')}</p>
}

src/pages/shared/UnpaidDeliveryButton.jsx

import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// Drop into the order modal in ComptoirDashboard (comptoir + admin).
// order = the selected order (needs id, final_price, price_confirmed, status)
export default function UnpaidDeliveryButton({ order, onDone }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [unpaid, setUnpaid] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  if (!order.price_confirmed) return null   // price must be confirmed first

  async function submit() {
    setError(null)
    setSaving(true)
    const { error: err } = await supabase.rpc('mark_order_unpaid', {
      p_order: order.id, p_unpaid: Number(unpaid), p_deliver: order.status !== 'delivered',
    })
    setSaving(false)
    if (err) { setError(err.message); return }
    setOpen(false)
    setUnpaid('')
    onDone?.()
  }

  return open ? (
    <div className="mt-3 p-3 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg bg-[#fff8f0]">
      <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">
        
        {t('Combien le client n\'a-t-il pas payé ? (sur un total de {total} DT)', { total: order.final_price })}
      </label>
      <input type="number" step="0.001" value={unpaid} onChange={(e) => setUnpaid(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm mb-2" />
      {error && <p className="text-[#b3170f] text-xs mb-2">{error}</p>}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button onClick={submit} disabled={saving}
          className="bg-[#c9750a] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
          {order.status === 'delivered' ? t('Enregistrer la dette') : t('Livrer et enregistrer la dette')}
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="text-xs px-3">{t('Annuler')}</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  ) : (
    <button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="mt-3 text-[#c9750a] text-sm font-semibold hover:underline">
      {order.status === 'delivered' ? t('⚠ Le client n\'a pas tout payé') : t('Livraison sans paiement complet')}
    </button>
  )
}

src/pages/shared/VoidRequestButton.jsx

import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

// "Void" button for one cash_transactions row. Comptoir sends a request with a
// reason (admin must accept). When the ADMIN presses it, the request is created
// and approved in the same click.
export default function VoidRequestButton({ tx, onDone }) {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const isAdmin = profile?.role === 'admin' || profile?.role === 'super_admin'

  async function submit() {
    setError(null)
    if (reason.trim().length < 3) {
      setError(t('Saisissez le motif de l\'annulation (3 caractères minimum)'))
      return
    }
    setSaving(true)
    const { data, error: e1 } = await supabase
      .from('void_requests')
      .insert({ cash_transaction_id: tx.id, reason: reason.trim(), requested_by: user.id })
      .select('id')
      .single()
    if (e1) {
      setSaving(false)
      setError(e1.message.includes('one_pending_void_per_tx')
        ? t('Une demande d\'annulation est déjà en attente pour cette opération')
        : t('Erreur :') + ' ' + e1.message)
      return
    }
    if (isAdmin) {
      const { error: e2 } = await supabase.rpc('review_void_request', {
        p_id: data.id, p_approve: true, p_note: t('Annulation directe par le gérant'),
      })
      if (e2) { setSaving(false); setError(t('Erreur :') + ' ' + e2.message); return }
    }
    setSaving(false)
    setOpen(false)
    setReason('')
    if (!isAdmin) alert(t('Demande d\'annulation envoyée, en attente de l\'accord du gérant'))
    onDone?.()
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} className="text-[#b3170f] text-xs font-semibold hover:underline">
        {isAdmin ? t('Annuler') : t('Demande d\'annulation')}
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center z-50" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 w-[380px]" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <h3 className="font-extrabold mb-1">{isAdmin ? t('Annuler l\'opération') : t('Demande d\'annulation d\'une opération')}</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-3">
              {tx.description} — {Number(tx.amount).toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}
            </p>
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold mb-1.5">{t('Motif de l\'annulation (obligatoire)')}</label>
            <textarea value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} rows={3}
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#e4211b]" />
            {error && <p className="text-[#b3170f] text-sm mt-2">{error}</p>}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-4">
              <button onClick={submit} disabled={saving}
                className="bg-[#e4211b] text-white text-sm font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
                {saving ? '...' : isAdmin ? t('Confirmer l\'annulation') : t('Envoyer la demande')}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="text-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[#e5e5e5]">
                
                {t('Retour')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

src/pages/caisse/Caisse.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import VoidRequestButton from '../shared/VoidRequestButton'
import { t, getLocale } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

const TYPE_LABELS = {
  repair_income: t('Revenu de réparation'),
  accessory_sale: t('Vendre un accessoire'),
  withdrawal: t('Retrait'),
  debt_collection: t('Encaissement d\'une dette'),
  loan_return: t('Avance remboursée'),
}

const SOURCE_LABELS = {
  repair_part: t('Dépense de pièce détachée'),
  general: t('Dépense générale'),
  loan_out: t('Avance prise sur la caisse'),
  debt_payment: t('Remboursement d\'une dette'),
}

const INCOME_TYPES = ['repair_income', 'accessory_sale', 'debt_collection', 'loan_return']
const isIncome = (tx) => INCOME_TYPES.includes(tx.type)

function labelOf(tx) {
  if (tx.type === 'expense_out') return SOURCE_LABELS[tx.source_type] || t('Dépense')
  // type stays "accessory_sale" for both regular accessories and phones —
  // source_type is what actually tells them apart
  if (tx.type === 'accessory_sale' && tx.source_type === 'phone_sale') return t('Vente d\'un téléphone')
  return TYPE_LABELS[tx.type] || tx.type
}

export default function Caisse() {
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('today') // today | month | all

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTransactions()
  }, [filter])

  async function loadTransactions() {
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    // "description" is written by the DB triggers (item + order number, or the
    // general expense text), so comptoir can read it without touching
    // repair_expenses / general_expenses (which RLS hides from them).
    let query = supabase
      .from('cash_transactions')
      .select('id, type, source_type, description, amount, created_at, profiles!cash_transactions_performed_by_fkey(full_name)')
      .eq('is_void', false) // voided rows never count
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })

    const now = new Date()
    if (filter === 'today') {
      query = query.gte('created_at', new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate()).toISOString())
    } else if (filter === 'month') {
      query = query.gte('created_at', new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1).toISOString())
    }

    const { data, error: qError } = await query
    if (qError) setError(qError.message)
    setTransactions(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const income = transactions.filter(isIncome).reduce((s, tx) => s + Number(tx.amount), 0)
  const expenses = transactions.filter((tx) => !isIncome(tx)).reduce((s, tx) => s + Number(tx.amount), 0)
  const net = income - expenses

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-end mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Caisse')}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Entrées et sorties de la caisse')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto -mx-1 px-1 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0">
          {[
            { key: 'today', label: t('Aujourd\'hui') },
            { key: 'month', label: t('Ce mois-ci') },
            { key: 'all', label: t('Tout') },
          ].map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
              className={`shrink-0 text-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-semibold ${
                filter === f.key
                  ? 'bg-[#1a1a1a] text-white'
                  : 'border border-[#e5e5e5] text-[#1a1a1a] hover:bg-[#f7f7f7]'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <p className="bg-[#fdeaea] text-[#b3170f] text-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-4">
          
          {t('Erreur de chargement :')} {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 sm:gap-4 mb-6">
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-1">{t('Entrées')}</p>
          <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#1f8a4c]">{income.toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-1">{t('Dépenses')}</p>
          <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#b3170f]">{expenses.toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5">
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-1">{t('Net')}</p>
          <p className={`text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold ${net >= 0 ? 'text-[#1f8a4c]' : 'text-[#b3170f]'}`}>
            {net.toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl">
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p>
        </div>
      ) : transactions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl">
          <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Aucune opération sur cette période')}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Phone / tablet: one card per transaction, nothing squeezed sideways */}
          <div className="lg:hidden flex flex-col gap-3">
            {transactions.map((tx) => {
              const inc = isIncome(tx)
              return (
                <div key={tx.id} className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-[#1a1a1a] text-sm">{labelOf(tx)}</p>
                      {tx.description && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mt-0.5 break-words">{tx.description}</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p className={`font-bold text-sm whitespace-nowrap ${inc ? 'text-[#1f8a4c]' : 'text-[#b3170f]'}`}>
                      {inc ? '+' : '-'}
                      {Number(tx.amount).toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[#f0f0ea] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">
                    <span>{tx.profiles?.full_name || '—'} · {new Date(tx.created_at).toLocaleString(getLocale())}</span>
                    <VoidRequestButton tx={tx} onDone={loadTransactions} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Desktop: the original table */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-[#faf9f5] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] font-semibold">
                  <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Type')}</th>
                  <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Détail')}</th>
                  <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Montant')}</th>
                  <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Par')}</th>
                  <th className="text-start px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">{t('Date')}</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {transactions.map((tx) => {
                  const inc = isIncome(tx)
                  return (
                    <tr key={tx.id} className="hover:bg-[#fbfaf6]">
                      <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">{labelOf(tx)}</td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">
                        {tx.description || '—'}
                      </td>
                      <td
                        className={`px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] font-bold ${
                          inc ? 'text-[#1f8a4c]' : 'text-[#b3170f]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {inc ? '+' : '-'}
                        {Number(tx.amount).toFixed(2)}  {t('DT')}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] text-[#6b6b6b]">
                        {tx.profiles?.full_name || '—'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4] text-xs text-[#6b6b6b]">
                        {new Date(tx.created_at).toLocaleString(getLocale())}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-[#ececE4]">
                        <VoidRequestButton tx={tx} onDone={loadTransactions} />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

5.13 — Admin pages

src/pages/admin/AdminDashboard.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient'
import LowStockAlert from '../shared/LowStockAlert'
import { t } from '../../i18n/LanguageContext'

const INCOME = ['repair_income', 'accessory_sale', 'debt_collection', 'loan_return']
const OUT = ['expense_out', 'withdrawal']
const money = (n) => t('{n} DT', { n: Number(n || 0).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '') })
const DAY = 24 * 3600 * 1000

function Card({ label, value, sub, to, tone = 'text-[#1a1a1a]' }) {
  const body = (
    <div className="bg-white border border-[#e5e5e5] rounded-xl p-4 h-full hover:bg-[#fbfaf6]">
      <p className="text-xs text-[#6b6b6b] mb-1">{label}</p>
      <p className={`text-xl font-extrabold ${tone}`}>{value}</p>
      {sub && <p className="text-[11px] text-[#9a9a9a] mt-1">{sub}</p>}
    </div>
  )
  return to ? <Link to={to}>{body}</Link> : body
}

// Admin home: today's money, devices in the workshop, things waiting for the admin.
export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const [d, setD] = useState(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState(null)

  async function load() {
    const start = new Date()
    start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
    const [tx, orders, voids, ge, re, debts] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('cash_transactions').select('type, amount').eq('is_void', false).gte('created_at', start.toISOString()),
      supabase.from('repair_orders').select('status, ready_at, created_at').in('status', ['received', 'diagnosing', 'in_repair', 'ready']),
      supabase.from('void_requests').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('status', 'pending'),
      supabase.from('general_expenses').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_approved', false),
      supabase.from('repair_expenses').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_approved', false),
      supabase.from('debts').select('kind, amount, paid_amount').eq('status', 'open'),
    ])
    const err = [tx, orders, voids, ge, re, debts].find((r) => r.error)
    if (err) { setError(err.error.message); return }

    const sum = (types) => (tx.data || []).filter((tx) => types.includes(tx.type)).reduce((s, tx) => s + Number(tx.amount), 0)
    const income = sum(INCOME)
    const out = sum(OUT)
    const o = orders.data || []
    const now = Date.now()
    const count = (s) => o.filter((x) => x.status === s).length
    const rem = (kinds) => (debts.data || []).filter((x) => kinds.includes(x.kind)).reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.amount) - Number(x.paid_amount), 0)

    setD({
      income, out, net: income - out,
      received: count('received'), diagnosing: count('diagnosing'), in_repair: count('in_repair'), ready: count('ready'),
      readyWaiting: o.filter((x) => x.status === 'ready' && x.ready_at && now - new Date(x.ready_at) > 2 * DAY).length,
      stuck: o.filter((x) => x.status !== 'ready' && now - new Date(x.created_at) > 3 * DAY).length,
      pending: (voids.count || 0) + (ge.count || 0) + (re.count || 0),
      owedToUs: rem(['client_unpaid', 'money_lent']),
      weOwe: rem(['supplier_unpaid']),
    })
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load()
    const t = setInterval(load, 60000)
    return () => clearInterval(t)
  }, [])

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e5e5]">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#1a1a1a] mb-1">{t('Tableau de bord')}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Résumé du jour et ce qui attend votre décision')}</p>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="bg-[#fdeaea] text-[#b3170f] text-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-3 mb-4">{t('Erreur de chargement :')} {error}</p>}
      <LowStockAlert />

      {!d ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-[#6b6b6b]">{t('Chargement...')}</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#e4211b] mb-2">{t('Caisse du jour')}</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-6">
            <Card label={t('Entrées')} value={money(d.income)} tone="text-[#1f8a4c]" to="/admin/reports/daily" />
            <Card label={t('Dépenses et retraits')} value={money(d.out)} tone="text-[#b3170f]" to="/admin/reports/daily" />
            <Card label={t('Net')} value={money(d.net)} tone={d.net >= 0 ? 'text-[#1f8a4c]' : 'text-[#b3170f]'} to="/caisse" />
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#e4211b] mb-2">{t('Appareils à l\'atelier')}</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-6">
            <Card label={t('Reçues')} value={d.received} to="/comptoir" />
            <Card label={t('En diagnostic / réparation')} value={d.diagnosing + d.in_repair} to="/comptoir" />
            <Card label={t('Prêtes à livrer')} value={d.ready} sub={d.readyWaiting ? t('dont {readyWaiting} attendent depuis plus de 2 jours', { readyWaiting: d.readyWaiting }) : null} tone="text-[#1f8a4c]" to="/comptoir" />
            <Card label={t('En retard (+3 jours)')} value={d.stuck} tone={d.stuck ? 'text-[#b3170f]' : 'text-[#1a1a1a]'} to="/comptoir" />
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#e4211b] mb-2">{t('Attend votre décision')}</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            <Card label={t('Demandes en attente d\'approbation')} value={d.pending} sub={t('Annulations + dépenses')} tone={d.pending ? 'text-[#b36b00]' : 'text-[#1a1a1a]'} to="/admin/expenses-approval" />
            <Card label={t('Dettes envers nous')} value={money(d.owedToUs)} to="/admin/debts" />
            <Card label={t('Dettes envers les autres')} value={money(d.weOwe)} to="/admin/debts" />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

src/pages/admin/AccessoriesManagement.jsx
