              <Route path="/admin/expenses" element={<LogRepairExpense />} />
              <Route path="/admin/general-expenses" element={<GeneralExpenses />} />
              <Route path="/admin/expenses-approval" element={<ExpensesApproval />} />
              <Route path="/admin/void-requests" element={<VoidRequests />} />
              <Route path="/admin/debts" element={<Debts />} />
              <Route path="/admin/clients" element={<Clients />} />
              <Route path="/admin/reports" element={<Reports />} />
              <Route path="/admin/reports/daily" element={<DailyReport />} />
            </Route>

            <Route path="/unauthorized" element={<Unauthorized />} />
          </Routes>
        </NotificationProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
    </LanguageProvider>
  )
}

Part 6 — Edge Functions and secrets

Two groups of server functions. They run at Supabase, not in the browser.

6.1 — Link the terminal to your project (once)

Replace YOUR-PROJECT-REF with the first part of your Supabase URL:

npx supabase login
npx supabase link --project-ref YOUR-PROJECT-REF

6.2 — Employee functions (add / edit / list / delete employees from the app)

They need no secret: Supabase gives them the project keys automatically.

supabase/functions/create-employee/index.ts

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.190.0/http/server.ts"
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2"

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
}
const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })

const ROLES = ["admin", "comptoir", "technicien"]
// accepts "Technicien", " technicien ", "technician"... and always returns the exact DB value
function cleanRole(r: unknown) {
  const v = String(r ?? "").trim().toLowerCase()
  if (v === "technician" || v === "tech") return "technicien"
  if (v === "counter") return "comptoir"
  return ROLES.includes(v) ? v : null
}

// returns { caller, callerRole } or a Response when not allowed
async function requireAdmin(req: Request) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization")
  if (!authHeader) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const sb = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!, {
    global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } },
  })
  const { data: { user }, error } = await sb.auth.getUser()
  if (error || !user) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const { data: p } = await sb.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user.id).single()
  if (!p || !["admin", "super_admin"].includes(p.role)) return json({ error: 'Seul l\'administrateur peut faire cela' }, 403)
  return { caller: user, callerRole: p.role as string }
}
// your project stores the key under the secret name SERVICE_ROLE_KEY; the built-in name is also accepted
const admin = () => createClient(
  Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
  (Deno.env.get("SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!,
)

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors })
  try {
    const g = await requireAdmin(req)
    if (g instanceof Response) return g

    const b = await req.json()
    const email = String(b.email ?? "").trim().toLowerCase()
    const password = String(b.password ?? "")
    const full_name = String(b.full_name ?? "").trim()
    const phone = String(b.phone ?? "").trim() || null
    const role = cleanRole(b.role)

    if (!email || !password || !full_name) return json({ error: 'Données manquantes' }, 400)
    if (!role) return json({ error: 'Rôle invalide : ' + String(b.role) }, 400)
    if (password.length < 6) return json({ error: 'Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins 6 caractères' }, 400)
    if (role === "admin" && g.callerRole !== "super_admin")
      return json({ error: 'Seul le Super Admin peut ajouter un compte admin' }, 403)

    const sa = admin()
    const { data: created, error: cErr } = await sa.auth.admin.createUser({
      email, password, email_confirm: true, user_metadata: { full_name, role },
    })
    if (cErr) return json({ error: cErr.message }, 400)

    // the trigger handle_new_user already created the profile with this role.
    // We only add the phone here (and the role only if it differs).
    const { data: prof } = await sa.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", created.user.id).maybeSingle()
    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = { phone }
    if (!prof) {
      await sa.from("profiles").insert({ id: created.user.id, full_name, role, phone })
    } else {
      if (prof.role !== role) patch.role = role
      const { error: uErr } = await sa.from("profiles").update(patch).eq("id", created.user.id)
      if (uErr) return json({ success: true, user_id: created.user.id, warning: uErr.message })
    }
    return json({ success: true, user_id: created.user.id })
  } catch (err) {
    return json({ error: (err as Error).message }, 500)
  }
})

supabase/functions/update-employee/index.ts

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.190.0/http/server.ts"
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2"

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
}
const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })

const ROLES = ["admin", "comptoir", "technicien"]
// accepts "Technicien", " technicien ", "technician"... and always returns the exact DB value
function cleanRole(r: unknown) {
  const v = String(r ?? "").trim().toLowerCase()
  if (v === "technician" || v === "tech") return "technicien"
  if (v === "counter") return "comptoir"
  return ROLES.includes(v) ? v : null
}

// returns { caller, callerRole } or a Response when not allowed
async function requireAdmin(req: Request) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization")
  if (!authHeader) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const sb = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!, {
    global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } },
  })
  const { data: { user }, error } = await sb.auth.getUser()
  if (error || !user) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const { data: p } = await sb.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user.id).single()
  if (!p || !["admin", "super_admin"].includes(p.role)) return json({ error: 'Seul l\'administrateur peut faire cela' }, 403)
  return { caller: user, callerRole: p.role as string }
}
// your project stores the key under the secret name SERVICE_ROLE_KEY; the built-in name is also accepted
const admin = () => createClient(
  Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
  (Deno.env.get("SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!,
)

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors })
  try {
    const g = await requireAdmin(req)
    if (g instanceof Response) return g

    const b = await req.json()
    const user_id = String(b.user_id ?? "")
    if (!user_id) return json({ error: 'user_id requis' }, 400)

    const sa = admin()
    const { data: target } = await sa.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user_id).single()
    if (!target) return json({ error: 'Employé introuvable' }, 404)
    if (["admin", "super_admin"].includes(target.role) && g.callerRole !== "super_admin" && user_id !== g.caller.id)
      return json({ error: 'Seul le Super Admin peut modifier un compte admin' }, 403)

    // ---- profile fields
    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    if (b.full_name !== undefined) {
      const n = String(b.full_name).trim()
      if (!n) return json({ error: 'Le nom est requis' }, 400)
      patch.full_name = n
    }
    if (b.phone !== undefined) patch.phone = String(b.phone).trim() || null
    if (b.role !== undefined) {
      const r = cleanRole(b.role)
      if (!r) return json({ error: 'Rôle invalide : ' + String(b.role) }, 400)
      if (r !== target.role) {
        if (user_id === g.caller.id) return json({ error: 'Vous ne pouvez pas changer votre propre rôle' }, 400)
        if ((r === "admin" || target.role === "admin") && g.callerRole !== "super_admin")
          return json({ error: 'Seul le Super Admin peut changer le rôle d\'un admin' }, 403)
        patch.role = r
      }
    }
    if (Object.keys(patch).length) {
      const { error } = await sa.from("profiles").update(patch).eq("id", user_id)
      if (error) return json({ error: error.message }, 400)
    }

    // ---- login fields (email / password)
    const auth: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    if (b.email !== undefined) {
      const e = String(b.email).trim().toLowerCase()
      if (!e) return json({ error: 'L\'e-mail est requis' }, 400)
      auth.email = e
      auth.email_confirm = true
    }
    if (b.password) {
      if (String(b.password).length < 6) return json({ error: 'Le mot de passe doit contenir au moins 6 caractères' }, 400)
      auth.password = String(b.password)
    }
    if (patch.full_name || patch.role) auth.user_metadata = { ...(patch.full_name ? { full_name: patch.full_name } : {}), ...(patch.role ? { role: patch.role } : {}) }
    if (Object.keys(auth).length) {
      const { error } = await sa.auth.admin.updateUserById(user_id, auth)
      if (error) return json({ error: error.message }, 400)
    }
    return json({ success: true })
  } catch (err) {
    return json({ error: (err as Error).message }, 500)
  }
})

supabase/functions/list-employees/index.ts

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.190.0/http/server.ts"
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2"

const cors = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
}
const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })

const ROLES = ["admin", "comptoir", "technicien"]

function cleanRole(r: unknown) {
  const v = String(r ?? "").trim().toLowerCase()
  if (v === "technician" || v === "tech") return "technicien"
  if (v === "counter") return "comptoir"
  return ROLES.includes(v) ? v : null
}

// returns { caller, callerRole } or a Response when not allowed
async function requireAdmin(req: Request) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization")
  if (!authHeader) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const sb = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!, {
    global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } },
  })
  const { data: { user }, error } = await sb.auth.getUser()
  if (error || !user) return json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, 401)
  const { data: p } = await sb.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user.id).single()
  if (!p || !["admin", "super_admin"].includes(p.role)) return json({ error: 'Seul l\'administrateur peut faire cela' }, 403)
  return { caller: user, callerRole: p.role as string }
}
// your project stores the key under the secret name SERVICE_ROLE_KEY; the built-in name is also accepted
const admin = () => createClient(
  Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
  (Deno.env.get("SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"))!,
)

// Returns { users: { [id]: email } } so the admin page can show each employee's login email.
// (Emails live in auth.users, which the browser cannot read directly.)
serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: cors })
  try {
    const g = await requireAdmin(req)
    if (g instanceof Response) return g
    const sa = admin()
    const map: Record<string, string> = {}
    for (let page = 1; page <= 20; page++) {
      const { data, error } = await sa.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 })
      if (error) return json({ error: error.message }, 400)
      for (const u of data.users) map[u.id] = u.email ?? ""
      if (data.users.length < 200) break
    }
    return json({ users: map })
  } catch (err) {
    return json({ error: (err as Error).message }, 500)
  }
})

supabase/functions/delete-employee/index.ts

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.190.0/http/server.ts"
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2"

const corsHeaders = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
}

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders })

  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization")!
    const supabaseClient = createClient(
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,
      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!,
      { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }
    )

    const { data: { user }, error: userError } = await supabaseClient.auth.getUser()
    if (userError || !user) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Non autorisé' }), { status: 401, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    const { data: callerProfile } = await supabaseClient.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user.id).single()

    if (!callerProfile || !["admin", "super_admin"].includes(callerProfile.role)) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Seul l\'administrateur peut supprimer des employés' }), { status: 403, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    const { user_id } = await req.json()
    if (!user_id) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'user_id requis' }), { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    if (user_id === user.id) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Vous ne pouvez pas supprimer votre propre compte' }), { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    const supabaseAdmin = createClient(Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!, Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!)

    const { data: targetProfile } = await supabaseAdmin.from("profiles").select("role").eq("id", user_id).single()

    if (targetProfile && ["admin", "super_admin"].includes(targetProfile.role) && callerProfile.role !== "super_admin") {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Seul le Super Admin peut supprimer un compte admin' }), { status: 403, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    const { error: deleteError } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.deleteUser(user_id)

    if (deleteError) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: deleteError.message }), { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true }), { status: 200, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
  } catch (err) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message }), { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } })
  }
})

Deploy them (from the project folder):

npx supabase functions deploy create-employee
npx supabase functions deploy update-employee
npx supabase functions deploy list-employees
npx supabase functions deploy delete-employee

6.3 — Phone push: make the keys

Run once (if it asks "Ok to proceed?" type y). It prints a Public Key and a Private Key:

npx web-push generate-vapid-keys

Public Key → goes in .env.local as VITE_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY and in Vercel (Part 8). Private Key → only in Supabase secrets (next step), never in Vercel, GitHub or any file. Save both in a note and never generate a new pair later (every phone that enabled notifications would stop receiving).

6.4 — Secrets in Supabase

Terminal way (or Supabase → Edge Functions → Secrets → Add new secret, 4 times, with these exact names):

npx supabase secrets set VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY=PASTE_PUBLIC VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=PASTE_PRIVATE VAPID_SUBJECT=mailto:you@example.com WEBHOOK_SECRET=PUT_LONG_RANDOM_TEXT

WEBHOOK_SECRET must be exactly the same text you put in section 9 of the database file (PUT_YOUR_WEBHOOK_SECRET). If you already ran the file with another text, edit section 9 and run only that section again.

6.5 — The push function

supabase/functions/send-push/index.ts

import { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2'
import webpush from 'npm:web-push@3.6.7'

// Called by the database trigger (Script R) every time a row is inserted into `notifications`.
// Finds the phones of the recipients and sends the Web Push. Dead subscriptions are deleted.

// The 3 secrets are cleaned before use. A key copied from a terminal or a dashboard often carries
// spaces, quotes, a line break or a trailing "=" — web-push then refuses it with
// "Vapid public key must be a URL safe Base 64 (without '=')" and the whole function crashes.
const clean = (v?: string | null) => (v ?? '').trim().replace(/^["']+|["']+$/g, '').trim()
const b64url = (v?: string | null) =>
  clean(v).replace(/=+$/, '').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_')

// If the keys are wrong we do NOT crash: we remember the reason and answer with it,
// so it shows up in plain words in `net._http_response` (and in the function logs).
let vapidError: string | null = null
try {
  const pub = b64url(Deno.env.get('VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY'))
  const priv = b64url(Deno.env.get('VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY'))
  if (pub.length !== 87) {
    throw new Error(`VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY has ${pub.length} characters, it must have 87 (it starts with B). Copy the "Public Key" line again.`)
  }
  if (priv.length !== 43) {
    throw new Error(`VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY has ${priv.length} characters, it must have 43. Copy the "Private Key" line again.`)
  }
  webpush.setVapidDetails(clean(Deno.env.get('VAPID_SUBJECT')), pub, priv)
} catch (e) {
  vapidError = String((e as Error).message ?? e)
  console.error('VAPID setup failed:', vapidError)
}

// the key is stored under SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (built in) or SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (your own secret)
const db = createClient(
  Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')!,
  (Deno.env.get('SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY'))!,
)

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  // Same cleaning on both sides, so a trailing space / line break / quotes saved with the secret
  // cannot cause a mismatch. Only the LENGTHS are written to the log (never the secrets themselves).
  const sent = clean(req.headers.get('x-webhook-secret'))
  const expected = clean(Deno.env.get('WEBHOOK_SECRET'))
  if (!expected || sent !== expected) {
    console.error(`403 forbidden: Script R sent ${sent.length} characters, WEBHOOK_SECRET has ${expected.length}` +
      (expected ? '' : ' (WEBHOOK_SECRET is not set)'))
    return new Response('forbidden', { status: 403 })
  }
  if (vapidError) return new Response(vapidError, { status: 500 })

  const { record: n } = await req.json()
  if (!n) return new Response('no record')

  let userIds: string[] = []
  if (n.recipient_id) userIds = [n.recipient_id]
  else if (n.recipient_role) {
    const roles = n.recipient_role === 'admin' ? ['admin', 'super_admin'] : [n.recipient_role]
    const { data } = await db.from('profiles').select('id').in('role', roles)
    userIds = (data ?? []).map((p: { id: string }) => p.id)
  }
  if (!userIds.length) return new Response('no recipients')

  const { data: subs } = await db.from('push_subscriptions').select('*').in('user_id', userIds)

  // each person gets the text in HIS language (profiles.lang: fr / en / ar, saved by the app; French by default).
  // Script S fills title_fr / title_en / message_fr / message_en; the Arabic original is the fallback.
  const { data: people } = await db.from('profiles').select('id, lang').in('id', userIds)
  const langOf = new Map((people ?? []).map((p: { id: string; lang: string | null }) => [p.id, p.lang === 'en' || p.lang === 'ar' ? p.lang : 'fr']))
  const payloadFor = (lang: string) => JSON.stringify({
    // Arabic = the original text written by the database triggers
    title: lang === 'ar' ? n.title : (lang === 'en' ? n.title_en : n.title_fr) || n.title_fr || n.title,
    body: lang === 'ar' ? n.message : (lang === 'en' ? n.message_en : n.message_fr) || n.message_fr || n.message,
    url: n.link_path || '/',
    tag: n.id,
  })

  await Promise.all((subs ?? []).map(async (s: any) => {
    try {
      await webpush.sendNotification(
        { endpoint: s.endpoint, keys: { p256dh: s.p256dh, auth: s.auth } },
        payloadFor(langOf.get(s.user_id) ?? 'fr'),
      )
    } catch (e: any) {
      // 404 / 410 = the phone removed the subscription: forget it. Anything else is written to the logs.
      if (e.statusCode === 404 || e.statusCode === 410) await db.from('push_subscriptions').delete().eq('id', s.id)
      else console.error('push failed', e.statusCode, String(e.body || e.message).slice(0, 200))
    }
  }))
  console.log('push sent to', (subs ?? []).length, 'device(s) for notification', n.id)
  return new Response('ok')
})

Deploy — the --no-verify-jwt part is required (the database proves itself with the secret header instead):

npx supabase functions deploy send-push --no-verify-jwt

Open the address it prints (usually http://localhost:5173). Log in with the admin from Part 3.

Part 8 — Put it online (GitHub + Vercel)

8.1 — Protect your keys

Make sure .env.local is listed in .gitignore (it is) and never commit it. If you pushed it by mistake: Supabase → Settings → API → rotate the anon key, update Vercel and your local file.

8.2 — Test the production build

npm run build

It must finish without errors (a "chunk larger than 500 kB" warning is normal).

8.3 — GitHub

  1. github.com → New repository → name smart-repair-shop → Private → Create (no README).
  2. First time on this computer only:
    git config --global user.name "Your Name"
git config --global user.email "your-email@example.com"
  3. In the project folder:
    git init
git add .
git commit -m "First version"
git branch -M main
git remote add origin https://github.com/USERNAME/smart-repair-shop.git
git push -u origin main
  4. Refresh the GitHub page: you must see src; you must not see .env.local or node_modules.

8.3b — Vercel

  1. vercel.com → Add New → Project → import the repository.
  2. Settings: Framework Preset Vite, Build Command npm run build, Output Directory dist, Install Command npm install.
  3. Before pressing Deploy, open Environment Variables and add (tick Production, Preview, Development):
    NameValue
    VITE_SUPABASE_URLhttps://YOUR-PROJECT-REF.supabase.co
    VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEYthe anon public key
    VITE_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEYthe VAPID Public Key (Part 6.3)
    VITE_PUBLIC_URLyour final address, e.g. https://mobile4you.vercel.app (no slash, no /login)
  4. Deploy. Note the address Vercel gives (Settings → Domains shows the short one). If a Vercel login page appears for visitors, turn off Deployment Protection for Production (Settings → Deployment Protection) — customers must open the tracking page without logging in.

VITE_ values are baked into the site when it is built: after changing one, Deployments → latest → ⋯ → Redeploy.

8.4 — Tell Supabase your address

Supabase → Authentication → URL Configuration: Site URL = your address (no slash at the end). Redirect URLs → add https://your-address/reset-password and https://your-address/account (keep the two localhost ones for testing). Save.

8.5 — Test live

Open the Vercel address, log in as admin, refresh on /admin (it must stay on the page — that proves vercel.json works). Then repeat tests 7–10 of Part 7, scanning the receipt QR with a real phone.

Part 9 — Phone push and installing the app

  1. Check that section 9 of the database file was run with the right project ref and the same secret as WEBHOOK_SECRET, and that send-push was deployed with --no-verify-jwt.
  2. Desktop Chrome first: open the live site, log in, tap the bell → Activez les notifications du téléphone → Allow. Check: select user_id, left(endpoint, 40), created_at from push_subscriptions; shows 1 row.
  3. Close the tab, run the bell test insert from Part 7: a system notification appears; click it → the site opens.
  4. Android: Chrome → ⋮ → Install app → open it → bell → enable → Allow.
  5. iPhone: Safari → Share → Add to Home Screen → open from the new icon (not from Safari) → bell → enable → Allow.
  6. Two employees on one phone: just log out and log in as the other; the phone follows the logged-in person.

Check that the database called the function

-- run a test insert first, wait 5 seconds, then:
select id, status_code, left(content, 120) as answer, created
from net._http_response order by created desc limit 5;
status_codeMeaning / fix
200The function ran. Edge Functions → send-push → Logs shows "push sent to N device(s)". N = 0: no phone saved for that role.
401"Verify JWT" is still on: redeploy with --no-verify-jwt.
403The secret in section 9 of the database file differs from WEBHOOK_SECRET. Set both again (a secret cannot be read back).
404send-push is not deployed.
500Read the function log; often a VAPID key copied wrongly.

Notifications only when the site is open → check the row above; use Chrome (not Brave); Do-not-disturb and battery savers hide push (set the browser to "Unrestricted" battery). Push never works on http://192.168…, only https. "This app cannot be installed": open /manifest.webmanifest, /sw.js, /icon-192.png on the live site — each must show the file; icon sizes must be exact.

Part 10 — Daily routine, troubleshooting, optional tools

Every time you change something

git add .
git commit -m "Short description of what changed"
git push

Vercel rebuilds by itself (about a minute).

Troubleshooting

ProblemFix
White page / 404 after refreshvercel.json missing or not pushed.
Login says "Failed to fetch" / "Invalid API key"The VITE_SUPABASE_… variables are missing or misspelled (locally in .env.local, live in Vercel then Redeploy).
"new row violates row-level security policy"A security rule is missing: check that the whole database file ran without error (Part 3.5), and that your user has the right role in profiles.
A technician does not see a deviceThe order is not assigned to him (admin/comptoir can reassign in Commandes).
Reset-password link says invalidSite URL / Redirect URLs missing (Part 8.4). One reset mail per minute per address.
Photos do not uploadThe product-images bucket is missing / not public, or the database file was run before it.
Bell does not update liveDatabase → Publications: notifications must be in supabase_realtime (section 5 of the database file).
Two push notifications per eventYou created a dashboard webhook and section 9. Keep one.
Arabic text looks mirrored wrongly somewhereSend a screenshot with the page name; these are small class fixes.

Facts to remember

  • Switching language reloads the page (on purpose, so every list re-reads the language).
  • Typed data (client names, notes, product names) is never translated. Colour names are saved in French.
  • Arabic readers see the original Arabic text of notifications; French/English readers see the translated version.
  • The comptoir's technician list shows technicians only (not admins); an order given to the admin still appears in the comptoir list.
  • The database refuses a technician who sets ready/delivered/cancelled; reception does that.

Optional — empty all the data and keep the logins

Orders, clients, stock, cash, debts, notifications are emptied and numbering restarts; tables, functions, rules, employees, settings and phones are kept. It cancels itself if a kept table would change. (Tested.)

-- ============================================================
-- OPTIONAL — empty all the DATA, keep the structure and the logins
-- Keeps: profiles (employees), app_settings, notif_phrases, push_subscriptions.
-- Everything else (orders, clients, stock, cash, debts, notifications...) is emptied
-- and the numbering restarts. Tables, functions, triggers and policies are NOT touched.
-- If anything would change in profiles, the whole script is cancelled (nothing is lost).
-- Product photos in Storage are not deleted (delete them in Storage if you want).
-- ============================================================
do $$
declare
  keep text[] := array['profiles','app_settings','notif_phrases','push_subscriptions'];
  tbls text;
  n_profiles bigint; n_settings bigint; n_phrases bigint; n_push bigint;
begin
  select count(*) into n_profiles from profiles;
  select count(*) into n_settings from app_settings;
  select count(*) into n_phrases  from notif_phrases;
  select count(*) into n_push     from push_subscriptions;

  select string_agg(format('%I.%I', schemaname, tablename), ', ') into tbls
  from pg_tables where schemaname = 'public' and tablename <> all(keep);

  execute 'truncate table ' || tbls || ' restart identity cascade';

  if (select count(*) from profiles) <> n_profiles
     or (select count(*) from app_settings) <> n_settings
     or (select count(*) from notif_phrases) <> n_phrases
     or (select count(*) from push_subscriptions) <> n_push then
    raise exception 'A kept table would have been emptied — cancelled, nothing was deleted';
  end if;
end $$;

-- check: profiles still there, orders gone
select (select count(*) from profiles) as profiles, (select count(*) from repair_orders) as orders;

If Supabase refuses to delete a row by hand ("violates foreign key constraint … is still referenced"), delete the child rows first, or use the script above.
